    for i = 2, #flat, 3 do
        real[#real + 1] = flat[i]
    end
    for i = 1, #real - 1, 2 do
        local v = real[i]
        real[i] = real[i + 1]
        real[i + 1] = v
    end
    for i = 1, #real do
        local v = real[i]
        real[i] = ((v % 16) * 16) + math.floor(v / 16)
    end
    local d = {}
    for i = 1, #real do
        local v = real[i]
        for j = #KB, 1, -1 do
            v = (v - KB[j]) % 256
            if v < 0 then
                v = v + 256
            end
        end
        for j = #KA, 1, -1 do
            v = (v - KA[j]) % 256
            if v < 0 then
                v = v + 256
            end
        end
        d[i] = v
    end
    local function X(a, b)
        local r, p = 0, 1
        while a > 0 or b > 0 do
            if a % 2 ~= b % 2 then
                r = r + p
            end
            a = math.floor(a / 2)
            b = math.floor(b / 2)
            p = p * 2
        end
        return r
    end

        local ks = s

            s2[i] = X(X(data[i], ks % 256), (i * 13 + 7) % 256)
            ks = (ks + t) % 256
